=== ./main.py ===

"""Điểm khởi động desktop, chạy: python main.py."""
import sys
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from app.ui.window import MainWindow
from app.ui.theme import STYLE


def main():
    """Khởi tạo Qt, stylesheet và vòng lặp sự kiện."""
    application = QApplication(sys.argv)
    application.setApplicationName("Minh Đăng Photo")
    application.setStyle("Fusion")
    application.setStyleSheet(STYLE)
    window = MainWindow()
    window.show()
    sys.exit(application.exec())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

  echo 'Chưa có môi trường .venv. Xem các lệnh cài đặt trong README.md.'
  exit 1
fi
exec .venv/bin/python activate_this.py


=== ./tests/test_enhancement_frequency.py ===

"""Kiểm thử đáp ứng tần số, khử vân tuần hoàn và mô hình khôi phục ảnh."""
import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from app.core import enhancement_frequency as frequency


def rgba_gray(gray):
    """Ghép ảnh mức xám thành RGBA đục để kiểm tra các đáp ứng số."""
    gray = np.asarray(gray, dtype=np.uint8)
    return np.dstack((gray, gray, gray, np.full(gray.shape, 255, dtype=np.uint8)))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('family', ['gaussian', 'butterworth', 'ideal'])
def test_low_pass_rejects_fine_sinusoid(family):
    """Thông thấp làm giảm dao động tần số cao trong vùng xa biên đệm."""
    x = np.arange(128)
    image = rgba_gray(np.tile(np.rint(128 + 40 * np.sin(2 * np.pi * x / 4)), (128, 1)))
    result = frequency.frequency_filter(image, family=family, cutoff=.025)
    original = image[32:-32, 32:-32, 0].astype(float)
    filtered = result[32:-32, 32:-32, 0].astype(float)
    # Lọc lý tưởng vẫn có gợn chậm do biên đệm; đo riêng sóng cần loại bỏ.
    amplitude = 2 * abs((filtered * np.sin(2 * np.pi * np.arange(32, 96) / 4)).mean())
    assert amplitude < 1
    assert filtered.std() < original.std() / 3
    assert abs(filtered.mean() - 128) < 5


def test_high_pass_retains_signed_response_and_boost_identity():
    """Ảnh thông cao có cả hai phía mức 128, boost hệ số 0 giữ nguyên ảnh."""
    gray = np.full((64, 64), 80, dtype=np.uint8)
    gray[:, 32:] = 180
    image = rgba_gray(gray)
    response = frequency.frequency_filter(image, pass_type='high', cutoff=.08)
    assert response[32, 31, 0] < 128 < response[32, 32, 0]
    assert abs(int(response[32, 10, 0]) - 128) <= 1
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(frequency.frequency_filter(image, pass_type='boost', amount=0), image)


def test_notch_removes_selected_periodic_stripes():
    """Cặp chắn tại ±16 chu kỳ/128 pixel loại phần lớn nhiễu vân dọc."""
    x = np.arange(128)
    image = rgba_gray(np.tile(np.rint(128 + 40 * np.cos(2 * np.pi * 16 * x / 128)), (128, 1)))
    result = frequency.notch_filter(image, offset_x=.125, offset_y=0, radius=.02)
    original = image[32:-32, 32:-32, 0].astype(float)
    filtered = result[32:-32, 32:-32, 0].astype(float)
    assert filtered.std() < original.std() / 4
    assert abs(filtered.mean() - 128) < 2


def test_notch_is_symmetric_for_opposite_offsets():
    """Đổi dấu tọa độ chỉ hoán đổi hai đỉnh chắn, không đổi kết quả thực."""
    image = rgba_gray(np.arange(256, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(16, 16))
    a = frequency.notch_filter(image, .18, -.14, .02)
    b = frequency.notch_filter(image, -.18, .14, .02)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(a, b)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('method', ['inverse', 'wiener'])
@pytest.mark.parametrize('psf', ['gaussian', 'motion'])
def test_delta_psf_restoration_has_no_shift_or_sign_error(method, psf):
    """PSF xung đơn vị và K=0 khôi phục đúng vị trí, không đảo hay dịch ảnh."""
    image = rgba_gray(np.arange(256, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(16, 16))
    result = frequency.restore(image, method=method, psf=psf, size=1, noise=0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, image)


def test_wiener_delta_psf_matches_known_gain():
    """Với H=1 và K=1, công thức Wiener cho đúng một nửa mức xám."""
    image = rgba_gray(np.full((8, 8), 120, dtype=np.uint8))
    result = frequency.restore(image, size=1, noise=1)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., :3], 60)


def test_wiener_reduces_error_when_gaussian_psf_matches():
    """Khôi phục làm giảm MSE khi mô hình PSF trùng phép mờ trên ảnh tổng hợp."""
    original = np.full((64, 64), 70, dtype=np.uint8)
    original[20:44, 20:44] = 180
    original[28:36, 28:36] = 110
    blurred = cv2.GaussianBlur(original, (5, 5), 1., borderType=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT_101)
    result = frequency.restore(rgba_gray(blurred), size=5, sigma=1., noise=.0001)[..., 0]
    before = np.mean((blurred.astype(float) - original) ** 2)
    after = np.mean((result.astype(float) - original) ** 2)
    assert after < before / 4


def test_spectrum_black_and_dc_peak():
    """Ảnh đen có phổ đen hữu hạn, ảnh hằng có đỉnh DC đúng tâm."""
    black = rgba_gray(np.zeros((16, 20), dtype=np.uint8))
    assert not np.any(frequency.spectrum(black)[..., :3])
    constant = rgba_gray(np.full((16, 20), 50, dtype=np.uint8))
    result = frequency.spectrum(constant)
    assert result[8, 10, 0] == 255
    assert np.count_nonzero(result[..., 0]) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('algorithm,params', [
    (frequency.spectrum, {}),
    (frequency.frequency_filter, {}),
    (frequency.frequency_filter, {'pass_type': 'high'}),
    (frequency.notch_filter, {}),
    (frequency.restore, {}),
    (frequency.homomorphic, {}),
])
def test_alpha_source_and_constant_or_transparent_are_safe(algorithm, params):
    """Mọi bộ lọc giữ alpha, dữ liệu nguồn và trả ảnh 8 bit hữu hạn."""
    image = np.full((13, 15, 4), 80, dtype=np.uint8)
    image[..., 3] = np.arange(15, dtype=np.uint8) * 17
    original = image.copy()
    result = algorithm(image, **params)
    assert result.shape == image.shape and result.dtype == np.uint8
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], image[..., 3])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(image, original)
    image[..., 3] = 0
    assert algorithm(image, **params).dtype == np.uint8


def test_homomorphic_unit_transfer_is_identity():
    """Hai hệ số bằng 1 tạo H=1, log và exp ngược nhau trong sai số 8 bit."""
    image = rgba_gray(np.arange(256, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(16, 16))
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(frequency.homomorphic(image, low_gain=1, high_gain=1), image)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function,params', [
    (frequency.frequency_filter, {'cutoff': 0}),
    (frequency.frequency_filter, {'cutoff': float('nan')}),
    (frequency.frequency_filter, {'family': 'unknown'}),
    (frequency.frequency_filter, {'order': 0}),
    (frequency.frequency_filter, {'pass_type': 'unknown'}),
    (frequency.notch_filter, {'offset_x': 0, 'offset_y': 0}),
    (frequency.notch_filter, {'radius': -1}),
    (frequency.restore, {'size': 2}),
    (frequency.restore, {'noise': -1}),
    (frequency.restore, {'epsilon': 0}),
    (frequency.restore, {'sigma': float('inf')}),
    (frequency.homomorphic, {'low_gain': 2, 'high_gain': 1}),
])
def test_invalid_parameters_raise(function, params):
    """Tham số không hợp lệ được báo rõ trước khi biến đổi Fourier."""
    image = rgba_gray(np.full((8, 8), 80, dtype=np.uint8))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        function(image, **params)


def test_fft_memory_budget_is_checked_before_allocation(monkeypatch):
    """Chặn khối FFT quá lớn trước cấp phát bộ nhớ của phép biến đổi."""
    monkeypatch.setattr(frequency, 'MAX_FFT_PIXELS', 100)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='FFT'):
        frequency.frequency_filter(rgba_gray(np.zeros((10, 10), dtype=np.uint8)))


=== ./tests/test_enhancement_point.py ===

"""Đối chiếu biến đổi điểm/histogram với ví dụ tính tay trong Chương 2."""
import numpy as np
import pytest

from app.core import enhancement_point as point


def rgba_levels(levels, alpha=255):
    """Dựng ảnh xám RGBA nhỏ từ dãy mức để kiểm tra kết quả tính tay."""
    values = np.asarray(levels, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(1, -1)
    return np.dstack((values, values, values, np.full_like(values, alpha)))


def test_chapter_log_gamma_and_negative_examples():
    """Ví dụ 2.4: r=50 lần lượt ra 205,181,113 qua âm bản/log/gamma0.5."""
    image = rgba_levels([50])
    assert point.negative(image)[0, 0, 0] == 205
    assert point.logarithm(image)[0, 0, 0] == 181
    assert point.gamma(image, .5)[0, 0, 0] == 113


def test_linear_clips_signed_values_without_absolute_value():
    """Bài giảng yêu cầu cộng b rồi clip; số âm không bị convertScaleAbs đảo dấu."""
    image = rgba_levels([20, 30, 210, 240])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.linear(image, gain=1.5, pivot=128)[0, :, 0], [0, 0, 251, 255])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.linear(image, bias=-40)[0, :, 0], [0, 0, 170, 200])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.linear(image, bias=40)[0, :, 0], [60, 70, 250, 255])


def test_piecewise_and_bunching_examples():
    """Giãn [80,170] cho125→128; bó B64 cho30,90,150,210→0,64,128,192."""
    image = rgba_levels([0, 80, 125, 170, 255])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.piecewise(image)[0, :, 0], [0, 0, 128, 255, 255])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.quantize(rgba_levels([30, 90, 150, 210]))[0, :, 0], [0, 64, 128, 192])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.piecewise(image, 0, 0, 255, 255), image)


def test_threshold_boundary_and_bit_planes():
    """Ngưỡng tay gồm dấu bằng; LSB/MSB đúng các bit của mã xám."""
    image = rgba_levels([0, 1, 127, 128, 129, 255])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.threshold(image, 128)[0, :, 0], [0, 0, 0, 255, 255, 255])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.bit_plane(image, 0)[0, :, 0], [0, 255, 255, 0, 255, 255])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.bit_plane(image, 7)[0, :, 0], [0, 0, 0, 255, 255, 255])


def test_equalization_uses_chapter_cdf_and_ignores_hidden_pixels():
    """CDF trực tiếp khác cdf-min OpenCV; pixel giấu không được tính vào phân bố."""
    image = rgba_levels([0, 0, 128, 255, 15])
    image[0, -1, 3] = 0
    result = point.equalize(image)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[0, :4, 0], [128, 128, 191, 255])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.equalize(rgba_levels([75, 75]))[0, :, 0], [255, 255])


def test_histogram_matching_discrete_inverse_cdf():
    """Đối sánh dùng ngưỡng CDF đích đầu tiên đạt CDF nguồn, giữ alpha nguồn."""
    source = rgba_levels([0, 255], alpha=128)
    reference = rgba_levels([50, 100, 150, 200, 0])
    reference[0, -1, 3] = 0
    result = point.match_histogram(source, reference)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[0, :, 0], [100, 200])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], source[..., 3])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.match_histogram(source, rgba_levels([123]))[0, :, 0], [123, 123])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        point.match_histogram(source, rgba_levels([0], alpha=0))


def test_otsu_hand_computed_six_level_histogram():
    """Bảng2.2 có n=[15,5,4,3,2,1], ngưỡng cực đại là t=1 và nền≤1."""
    levels = np.repeat(np.arange(6), [15, 5, 4, 3, 2, 1])
    image = rgba_levels(np.append(levels, 255))
    image[0, -1, 3] = 0
    result, threshold = point.otsu(image)
    assert threshold == 1
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[0, :30, 0], np.where(levels > 1, 255, 0))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function', [point.linear, point.negative, point.logarithm,
    point.gamma, point.piecewise, point.threshold, point.bit_plane, point.quantize,
    point.equalize, point.clahe, lambda image: point.match_histogram(image, rgba_levels([20, 200]))])
def test_point_operations_preserve_source_and_alpha(function, rgba):
    """Mỗi phép trả RGBA mới, giữ alpha kể cả bán trong suốt và không sửa nguồn."""
    before = rgba.copy()
    result = function(rgba)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(rgba, before)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], rgba[..., 3])
    assert result.shape == rgba.shape and result.dtype == np.uint8
    assert not np.shares_memory(result, rgba)


def test_transparent_statistics_and_clahe_ignore_hidden_rgb():
    """Ảnh hoàn toàn trong suốt an toàn; RGB giấu không gây thay đổi CLAHE thấy được."""
    transparent = rgba_levels([20, 100, 200], alpha=0)
    for function in (point.equalize, point.clahe):
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(function(transparent), transparent)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.otsu(transparent)[0], transparent)
    first = np.tile(rgba_levels([25, 75, 125, 175]), (4, 1, 1))
    first[:, 3, 3] = 0
    second = first.copy()
    second[:, 3, :3] = [255, 0, 255]
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(point.clahe(first, tiles=2)[..., :3], point.clahe(second, tiles=2)[..., :3])


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function', [
    lambda image: point.linear(image, gain=np.nan),
    lambda image: point.logarithm(image, gain=-1),
    lambda image: point.gamma(image, gamma=0),
    lambda image: point.gamma(image, gamma=np.inf),
    lambda image: point.piecewise(image, 80, 0, 80, 255),
    lambda image: point.piecewise(image, 0, 5, 200, 255),
    lambda image: point.threshold(image, 256),
    lambda image: point.bit_plane(image, 8),
    lambda image: point.bit_plane(image, 1.5),
    lambda image: point.quantize(image, 0),
    lambda image: point.clahe(image, tiles=0),
    lambda image: point.clahe(image, clip_limit=np.nan),
])
def test_invalid_point_parameters_raise(function):
    """Tham số sai bị chặn trước khi NumPy/OpenCV trả kết quả khó đoán."""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        function(rgba_levels([50]))


=== ./tests/conftest.py ===

"""Fixture ảnh tổng hợp có gradient, nhiễu và alpha biến đổi."""
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path
os.environ.setdefault('QT_QPA_PLATFORM', 'offscreen')
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
import numpy as np
import pytest


@pytest.fixture
def rgba():
    """Ảnh kiểm thử xác định được nhờ random seed cố định."""
    image = np.random.default_rng(42).integers(0, 256, (48, 64, 4), dtype=np.uint8)
    image[..., 3] = np.arange(64, dtype=np.uint8)[None, :] * 4
    return image


=== ./tests/test_enhancement_spatial.py ===

"""Ví dụ số Chương 2 và các trường hợp biên của lọc không gian, chỉ số chất lượng."""
import numpy as np
import pytest

from app.core import enhancement_spatial as spatial


def rgba_gray(values):
    """Tạo RGBA từ ma trận xám, giữ nguyên giá trị để tính tay bộ lọc."""
    gray = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.uint8)
    return np.dstack((np.repeat(gray[..., None], 3, axis=2), np.full(gray.shape, 255, dtype=np.uint8)))


@pytest.fixture
def chapter_matrix():
    """Ma trận 5×5 trang 33: điểm nhiễu 50 ở biên nền 10/100."""
    image = rgba_gray([[10, 10, 10, 100, 100]] * 5)
    image[2, 2, :3] = 50
    return image


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind,expected', [('mean', 44), ('gaussian', 43), ('median', 10)])
def test_textbook_smoothing_center(chapter_matrix, kind, expected):
    """Tổng 400/9, Gauss nhị thức 680/16 và trung vị 10 khớp ví dụ 2.8–2.10."""
    result = spatial.smooth(chapter_matrix, kind, size=3, sigma=0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[2, 2, :3], [expected] * 3)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('kind', ['mean', 'gaussian', 'median'])
def test_smoothing_uses_reflected_border(kind):
    """Góc ảnh dùng REFLECT_101, khác quy ước lặp pixel biên mặc định của median."""
    image = rgba_gray([[1, 2, 100], [3, 4, 100], [100, 100, 100]])
    result = spatial.smooth(image, kind, size=3, sigma=0)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[0, 0, :3], [3] * 3)


def test_k_nearest_center_and_strict_threshold():
    """K=3 lấy 16,4,4 thành 8; ngưỡng bằng sai khác không thay pixel."""
    image = rgba_gray([[1, 2, 3], [2, 16, 4], [2, 1, 4]])
    assert spatial.nearest_mean(image)[1, 1, 0] == 8
    assert spatial.nearest_mean(image, threshold=8)[1, 1, 0] == 16
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(spatial.nearest_mean(image, neighbors=1), image)


def test_k_nearest_tiling_matches_whole_window_result():
    """Ảnh rộng vượt giới hạn khối vẫn được lọc liên tục tại đường nối hai khối."""
    row = np.arange(256, dtype=np.uint8)
    values = np.tile(row, 9)[None, :]
    image = rgba_gray(np.repeat(values, 3, axis=0))
    # Cửa sổ 31 với K=1 luôn là mức của chính tâm, kể cả hai phía ranh giới khối.
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(spatial.nearest_mean(image, size=31, neighbors=1), image)


def test_laplacian_four_neighbors_and_sobel_clip(chapter_matrix):
    """Laplace=-70 nên f−Laplace=120; Sobel Gx=360 clip255 và Gy=0."""
    assert spatial.laplacian(chapter_matrix)[2, 2, 0] == 120
    assert spatial.laplacian(chapter_matrix, edges_only=True)[2, 2, 0] == 70
    assert spatial.sobel(chapter_matrix, 'x')[2, 2, 0] == 255
    assert spatial.sobel(chapter_matrix, 'y')[2, 2, 0] == 0
    assert spatial.sobel(chapter_matrix, 'magnitude')[2, 2, 0] == 255


def test_unsharp_uses_float_mask_and_zero_amount_identity(chapter_matrix):
    """High-boost tính từ Gauss chính xác 42.5 trước khi clip, không tràn uint8."""
    result = spatial.unsharp(chapter_matrix, sigma=0, size=3, amount=2)
    assert result[2, 2, 0] == 65
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(spatial.unsharp(chapter_matrix, amount=0), chapter_matrix)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(spatial.laplacian(chapter_matrix, amount=0), chapter_matrix)


def test_adaptive_median_grows_window_and_keeps_valid_center():
    """Bước A mở cửa sổ 3 lên 5; bước B giữ điểm hợp lệ dù khác trung vị."""
    image = rgba_gray(np.full((5, 5), 100, dtype=np.uint8))
    image[1:4, 1:4, :3] = 255
    image[2, 2, :3] = 0
    assert spatial.adaptive_median(image, max_size=3)[2, 2, 0] == 255
    assert spatial.adaptive_median(image, max_size=5)[2, 2, 0] == 100
    valid = rgba_gray([[0, 30, 30], [30, 20, 30], [30, 30, 255]])
    assert spatial.adaptive_median(valid, max_size=3)[1, 1, 0] == 20


@pytest.mark.parametrize('shape', [(1, 1), (1, 4), (4, 1), (4, 5)])
def test_filters_handle_tiny_constant_images(shape):
    """Ảnh hằng và một hàng/cột không sinh NaN hoặc lỗi đệm biên."""
    image = rgba_gray(np.full(shape, 80, dtype=np.uint8))
    for result in [spatial.smooth(image), spatial.smooth(image, 'median', 7),
                   spatial.nearest_mean(image, size=5), spatial.adaptive_median(image),
                   spatial.unsharp(image), spatial.laplacian(image)]:
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, image)
    assert not np.any(spatial.sobel(image)[..., :3])


def test_average_streaming_weights_alpha_and_preserves_first_alpha():
    """Giá trị RGB ẩn không pha vào ảnh; alpha bán trong suốt có trọng số đúng."""
    first = np.array([[[0, 20, 40, 255], [250, 250, 250, 0], [20, 40, 60, 128]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    second = np.array([[[100, 120, 140, 255], [10, 20, 30, 255], [80, 100, 120, 128]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    result = spatial.average_images(image for image in [first, second])
    expected = np.array([[[50, 70, 90, 255], [10, 20, 30, 0], [50, 70, 90, 128]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, expected)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(first[0, 0], [0, 20, 40, 255])
    opaque = np.array([[[0, 0, 0, 255]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    partial = np.array([[[255, 255, 255, 85]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    assert spatial.average_images([opaque, partial])[0, 0, 0] == 64


@pytest.mark.parametrize('images', [[], [rgba_gray([[10]])], [rgba_gray([[10]]), rgba_gray([[10, 20]])]])
def test_average_rejects_missing_or_mismatched_frames(images):
    """Cần ít nhất hai khung cùng kích thước để cộng dồn cảnh tĩnh."""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        spatial.average_images(iter(images))


def test_quality_metrics_identity_and_known_error():
    """Sai khác hằng 10 cho MSE100 và PSNR28.1308; ảnh trùng có SSIM1."""
    image = rgba_gray(np.full((12, 13), 80, dtype=np.uint8))
    identity = spatial.quality_metrics(image, image.copy())
    assert identity['mse'] == 0 and np.isinf(identity['psnr'])
    assert identity['ssim'] == pytest.approx(1)
    reference = rgba_gray(np.full((12, 13), 90, dtype=np.uint8))
    metrics = spatial.quality_metrics(image, reference)
    assert metrics['mse'] == 100
    assert metrics['psnr'] == pytest.approx(28.1308036087)
    c1 = (.01 * 255) ** 2
    assert metrics['ssim'] == pytest.approx((2 * 80 * 90 + c1) / (80 ** 2 + 90 ** 2 + c1))


def test_quality_ignores_hidden_rgb_even_in_local_ssim_windows():
    """Thay RGB phía alpha=0 không ảnh hưởng MSE và cả cửa sổ Gaussian SSIM."""
    reference = rgba_gray(np.arange(25, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(5, 5) * 8)
    image = reference.copy()
    image[0, 0, :3] += 10
    image[1:3, 1:3, 3] = 0
    metrics = spatial.quality_metrics(image, reference)
    image[1:3, 1:3, :3] = 255
    assert spatial.quality_metrics(image, reference) == pytest.approx(metrics)
    reference[4, 4, 3] = 0
    image[4, 4, :3] = 255
    metrics = spatial.quality_metrics(image, reference)
    image[4, 4, :3] = 0
    assert spatial.quality_metrics(image, reference) == pytest.approx(metrics)


def test_quality_tiny_image_and_no_visible_intersection():
    """SSIM trên một pixel hữu hạn; hai ảnh không giao alpha nhìn thấy bị từ chối."""
    metrics = spatial.quality_metrics(rgba_gray([[0]]), rgba_gray([[255]]))
    assert metrics['mse'] == 65025 and metrics['psnr'] == 0
    assert np.isfinite(metrics['ssim'])
    hidden = rgba_gray([[40]])
    hidden[..., 3] = 0
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='nhìn thấy'):
        spatial.quality_metrics(hidden, rgba_gray([[40]]))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='kích thước'):
        spatial.quality_metrics(rgba_gray([[40]]), rgba_gray([[40, 40]]))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function,kwargs', [
    (spatial.smooth, {'kind': 'unknown'}), (spatial.smooth, {'size': 4}),
    (spatial.smooth, {'sigma': np.nan}), (spatial.nearest_mean, {'neighbors': 0}),
    (spatial.nearest_mean, {'neighbors': 10}), (spatial.nearest_mean, {'threshold': -1}),
    (spatial.sobel, {'direction': 'z'}), (spatial.laplacian, {'amount': np.inf}),
    (spatial.unsharp, {'sigma': 0, 'size': 0}), (spatial.unsharp, {'size': 4}),
    (spatial.adaptive_median, {'max_size': 33}), (spatial.pipeline, {'clip_limit': 0}),
    (spatial.pipeline, {'tiles': 0}), (spatial.pipeline, {'sigma': np.nan})])
def test_invalid_spatial_parameters_report_value_error(chapter_matrix, function, kwargs):
    """Tham số sai được chặn rõ ràng trước khi gọi OpenCV."""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        function(chapter_matrix, **kwargs)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function', [spatial.smooth, spatial.nearest_mean, spatial.sobel,
                                     spatial.laplacian, spatial.unsharp, spatial.adaptive_median,
                                     spatial.pipeline])
def test_rgba_contract_and_input_immutability(rgba, function):
    """Mọi bộ lọc giữ đúng dtype/kích thước/alpha và không sửa mảng nguồn."""
    original = rgba.copy()
    result = function(rgba)
    assert result.dtype == np.uint8 and result.shape == rgba.shape
    assert np.isfinite(result).all()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], original[..., 3])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(rgba, original)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        function(rgba[..., :3])


=== ./tests/test_analysis_morphology.py ===

"""Ví dụ Chương 3 và tính chất tập hợp của các phép toán hình thái học."""
import cv2
import numpy as np
import pytest

from app.core import analysis_morphology as morph


def rgba_gray(values):
    gray = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.uint8)
    return np.dstack((gray, gray, gray, np.full(gray.shape, 255, dtype=np.uint8)))


def binary_image(values):
    return rgba_gray(np.asarray(values, dtype=np.uint8) * 255)


def test_textbook_cross_dilation_and_erosion():
    """Khối 3×3 của trang 40–41: giãn ra 21 ô, co còn đúng tâm."""
    image = binary_image(np.pad(np.ones((3, 3), dtype=np.uint8), 1))
    dilation = morph.morphology(image, 'dilate')[..., 0] > 0
    expected = np.ones((5, 5), dtype=bool)
    expected[[0, 0, 4, 4], [0, 4, 0, 4]] = False
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(dilation, expected)
    erosion = morph.morphology(image, 'erode')[..., 0] > 0
    expected[:] = False
    expected[2, 2] = True
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(erosion, expected)


def test_open_removes_isolated_speck_close_fills_small_hole_boundary_subtracts_erosion():
    gray = np.zeros((11, 11), dtype=np.uint8)
    gray[3:8, 3:8] = 255
    gray[1, 1] = 255
    image = rgba_gray(gray)
    opened = morph.morphology(image, 'open', shape='rect')
    assert opened[1, 1, 0] == 0
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(opened[3:8, 3:8, 0], np.full((5, 5), 255))
    image[5, 5, :3] = 0
    closed = morph.morphology(image, 'close', shape='rect')
    assert closed[5, 5, 0] == 255
    assert closed[1, 1, 0] == 255
    boundary = morph.morphology(opened, 'boundary', shape='rect')
    assert boundary[3, 3, 0] == 255 and boundary[5, 5, 0] == 0
    assert np.count_nonzero(boundary[..., 0]) == 16


def test_open_idempotence_and_containment_close_includes_objects_at_image_border():
    rng = np.random.default_rng(7)
    image = binary_image(rng.integers(0, 2, (17, 19), dtype=np.uint8))
    opened = morph.morphology(image, 'open', shape='rect')
    closed = morph.morphology(image, 'close', shape='rect')
    assert np.all(opened[..., 0] <= image[..., 0])
    assert np.all(closed[..., 0] >= image[..., 0])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(morph.morphology(opened, 'open', shape='rect'), opened)
    # Đệm ngoài đủ rộng: phép đóng không làm mất hàng trắng sát mép.
    full = binary_image(np.ones((3, 5), dtype=np.uint8))
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(morph.morphology(full, 'close', size=5), full)


def test_kernel_shapes_and_iterations_are_effective():
    image = binary_image(np.pad(np.ones((1, 1), dtype=np.uint8), 3))
    assert np.count_nonzero(morph.morphology(image, shape='cross')[..., 0]) == 5
    assert np.count_nonzero(morph.morphology(image, shape='rect')[..., 0]) == 9
    assert np.count_nonzero(morph.morphology(image, shape='ellipse')[..., 0]) == 5
    assert np.count_nonzero(morph.morphology(image, iterations=2)[..., 0]) == 13


def test_fill_all_closed_holes_preserves_open_and_transparent_background():
    gray = np.zeros((11, 13), dtype=np.uint8)
    gray[1:10, 1:12] = 255
    gray[3:5, 3:5] = 0
    gray[6:8, 8:10] = 0
    gray[1:4, 7] = 0  # Lối mở ra ngoài không được lấp.
    image = rgba_gray(gray)
    filled = morph.fill_holes(image)
    assert np.all(filled[3:5, 3:5, 0] == 255)
    assert np.all(filled[6:8, 8:10, 0] == 255)
    assert np.all(filled[1:4, 7, 0] == 0)
    image[3, 3, 3] = 0  # Một lỗ có alpha=0 được coi là miền nền ngoài.
    assert np.all(morph.fill_holes(image)[3:5, 3:5, 0] == 0)


def test_fill_holes_connectivity_decides_whether_diagonal_gap_is_open():
    gray = np.full((5, 5), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    gray[[0, 1, 2], [0, 1, 2]] = 0
    image = rgba_gray(gray)
    assert morph.fill_holes(image, connectivity=4)[2, 2, 0] == 255
    assert morph.fill_holes(image, connectivity=8)[2, 2, 0] == 0


def test_grayscale_min_max_and_top_hat_preserve_constant_background():
    image = rgba_gray(np.full((7, 7), 80, dtype=np.uint8))
    image[3, 3, :3] = 200
    assert morph.morphology(image, 'erode', grayscale=True)[3, 3, 0] == 80
    assert morph.morphology(image, 'dilate', grayscale=True)[3, 2, 0] == 200
    top_hat = morph.morphology(image, 'top_hat', grayscale=True)
    assert top_hat[3, 3, 0] == 120
    assert np.count_nonzero(top_hat[..., 0]) == 1
    image[0, 0, 3] = 0
    eroded = morph.morphology(image, 'erode', grayscale=True)
    assert eroded[0, 1, 0] == 80  # Alpha=0 không trở thành số 0 kéo tối láng giềng.


@pytest.mark.parametrize('rotation,corner', [(0, (2, 2)), (90, (6, 2)), (180, (6, 6)), (270, (2, 6))])
def test_hit_or_miss_finds_requested_corner(rotation, corner):
    image = binary_image(np.pad(np.ones((5, 5), dtype=np.uint8), 2))
    result = morph.hit_or_miss(image, rotation=rotation)[..., 0]
    assert result[corner] == 255
    assert np.count_nonzero(result) == 1


@pytest.mark.parametrize('shape', ['rect', 'cross', 'ellipse'])
def test_distance_skeleton_matches_definition_by_successive_erosion(shape):
    """Đối chiếu công thức hợp (co−mở) độc lập với cách tối ưu bằng khoảng cách."""
    gray = np.zeros((13, 15), dtype=np.uint8)
    gray[1:12, 2:13] = 255
    gray[0, 0] = 255
    gray[2, 8] = 0
    image = rgba_gray(gray)
    kernels = {'rect': cv2.MORPH_RECT, 'cross': cv2.MORPH_CROSS, 'ellipse': cv2.MORPH_ELLIPSE}
    kernel = cv2.getStructuringElement(kernels[shape], (3, 3))
    current = gray.copy()
    expected = np.zeros_like(gray)
    while current.any():
        eroded = cv2.erode(current, kernel, borderType=cv2.BORDER_CONSTANT, borderValue=0)
        opened = cv2.dilate(eroded, kernel, borderType=cv2.BORDER_CONSTANT, borderValue=0)
        expected |= cv2.subtract(current, opened)
        current = eroded
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(morph.skeleton(image, shape=shape)[..., 0], expected)


def test_threshold_invert_and_hidden_pixels_are_excluded_from_foreground():
    image = rgba_gray([[0, 127, 128, 255]])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(morph.morphology(image, size=1)[0, :, 0], [0, 0, 255, 255])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(morph.morphology(image, size=1, invert=True)[0, :, 0], [255, 255, 0, 0])
    image = rgba_gray([[0, 255, 0], [0, 255, 0], [0, 0, 0]])
    image[:2, 1, 3] = 0
    assert not morph.morphology(image, 'dilate')[..., :3].any()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('shape', [(1, 1), (1, 5), (5, 1)])
def test_tiny_images(shape):
    image = rgba_gray(np.full(shape, 200, dtype=np.uint8))
    for operation in ('dilate', 'erode', 'open', 'close'):
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(morph.morphology(image, operation, grayscale=True), image)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(morph.morphology(image, 'close'), binary_image(np.ones(shape)))
    assert morph.fill_holes(image).shape == image.shape
    assert morph.hit_or_miss(image).shape == image.shape
    assert morph.skeleton(image).shape == image.shape


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function', [morph.morphology, morph.fill_holes, morph.hit_or_miss, morph.skeleton])
def test_rgba_immutability_hidden_colors_and_validation(rgba, function):
    original = rgba.copy()
    result = function(rgba)
    assert result.shape == rgba.shape and result.dtype == np.uint8
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], rgba[..., 3])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(rgba, original)
    changed = rgba.copy()
    changed[changed[..., 3] == 0, :3] = 255
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(function(changed), result)
    hidden = rgba.copy()
    hidden[..., 3] = 0
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(function(hidden), hidden)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        function(rgba[..., :3])


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function,kwargs', [
    (morph.morphology, {'size': 2}), (morph.morphology, {'shape': 'triangle'}),
    (morph.morphology, {'operation': 'other'}), (morph.morphology, {'iterations': 0}),
    (morph.morphology, {'iterations': 1.2}), (morph.morphology, {'grayscale': 'yes'}),
    (morph.morphology, {'threshold': np.nan}), (morph.fill_holes, {'threshold': 256}),
    (morph.fill_holes, {'connectivity': 6}), (morph.fill_holes, {'invert': 1}),
    (morph.hit_or_miss, {'rotation': 45}), (morph.skeleton, {'shape': 'unknown'}),
])
def test_invalid_parameters(function, kwargs):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        function(rgba_gray([[40]]), **kwargs)


=== ./tests/test_ui.py ===

"""Kiểm thử tích hợp Qt offscreen; không thay cho thao tác trên cửa sổ thật."""
import time
import numpy as np
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from PySide6.QtCore import QPoint, Qt
from PySide6.QtTest import QTest
from app.ui.window import MainWindow
from app.core import geometry


def wait_done(app, window):
    """Bơm event loop tới khi worker hoàn tất hoặc hết thời gian."""
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 15
    while window.busy and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        app.processEvents()
        QTest.qWait(10)
    assert not window.busy


def test_ui_edit_compare_and_undo(rgba):
    """Slider preview, worker commit, undo/redo và compare giữ mask."""
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    w = MainWindow(); w.show(); app.processEvents()
    w.set_document(rgba, 'test.png')
    errors = []
    w.alert = errors.append
    w.sliders['brightness'].setValue(20)
    w.preview()
    assert w.pending() and not w.doc.dirty
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(w.doc.current, rgba)
    w.apply_adjustments()
    wait_done(app, w)
    assert not w.pending() and w.doc.dirty and not errors
    changed = w.doc.current.copy()
    w.undo(); np.testing.assert_array_equal(w.doc.current, rgba)
    w.redo(); np.testing.assert_array_equal(w.doc.current, changed)
    w.commit(geometry.crop(changed, (0, 0, 32, 24)), 'crop')
    w.canvas.mask[4:8, 4:8] = 255
    before = w.canvas.mask.copy()
    w.show_original(True); w.show_original(False)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(w.canvas.mask, before)
    w.doc.mark_saved(); w.close()


def test_canvas_mouse_mapping(rgba):
    """Crop et cọ dùng pixel ảnh dù viewport đang fit/zoom."""
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    w = MainWindow(); w.show(); app.processEvents()
    w.set_document(np.tile(rgba, (4, 4, 1)), 'mouse.png')
    app.processEvents(); w.canvas.fit_image()
    from PySide6.QtCore import QPointF
    p1 = w.canvas.mapFromScene(QPointF(30, 30))
    p2 = w.canvas.mapFromScene(QPointF(90, 80))
    w.mode('crop')
    QTest.mousePress(w.canvas.viewport(), Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton, pos=p1)
    QTest.mouseMove(w.canvas.viewport(), p2)
    QTest.mouseRelease(w.canvas.viewport(), Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton, pos=p2)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(w.canvas.crop_box(), (30, 30, 90, 80), atol=1)
    w.mode('brush')
    w.canvas.brush_size = 10
    QTest.mousePress(w.canvas.viewport(), Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton, pos=p1)
    QTest.mouseMove(w.canvas.viewport(), p2)
    QTest.mouseRelease(w.canvas.viewport(), Qt.MouseButton.LeftButton, pos=p2)
    assert w.canvas.mask[30, 30] > 0 and w.canvas.mask[80, 90] > 0
    assert w.canvas.mask[0, 0] == 0
    w.close()


def test_tool_dispatch_and_failure(rgba, monkeypatch):
    """Các dialog gọi đúng core; worker lỗi giữ nguyên ảnh và mở lại UI."""
    import app.ui.window as module
    app = QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])
    w = MainWindow(); w.set_document(rgba, 'tools.png'); errors=[]; w.alert=errors.append
    tools = [
        (w.do_curve, {'channel': 'RGB', 'points': '0:0, 128:150, 255:255'}),
        (w.do_hsl, {'center': 'Đỏ', 'hue': 20, 'sat': 10, 'light': 5}),
        (w.do_filter, {'kind': 'CLAHE', 'amount': 10}),
        (w.do_vignette, {'amount': 30}),
        (w.do_rotate, {'degrees': 15}),
        (w.do_resize, {'w': 40, 'h': 30, 'ratio': 'Giữ tỉ lệ theo chiều rộng'}),
        (w.ratio_crop, {'ratio': '1:1'}),
        (w.do_border, {'size': 3, 'color': '#fff'})]
    for method, params in tools:
        monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'parameters', lambda *args, p=params, **kwargs: p)
        method(); wait_done(app, w)
    assert not errors and len(w.doc.entries) == 9
    before=w.doc.current.copy()
    w.run_image('Lỗi kiểm thử', lambda image: geometry.resize(image, 0, 0))
    wait_done(app, w)
    assert len(errors) == 1 and w.centralWidget().isEnabled()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(w.doc.current, before)
    w.doc.mark_saved(); w.close()


=== ./tests/test_adjustments_accuracy.py ===

"""Known color examples catch differences between HSL, HSV and gamma conventions."""
import numpy as np
import pytest

from app.core import adjustments, enhancement_point


@pytest.mark.parametrize('saturation,lightness,expected', [
    (0, 25, [255, 128, 128]),
    (0, 50, [255, 255, 255]),
    (0, -50, [0, 0, 0]),
    (-100, 0, [128, 128, 128]),
])
def test_hsl_red_uses_lightness_and_hsl_saturation(saturation, lightness, expected):
    """Red has HSL L=0.5; lightening must work even when HSV V is already 1."""
    source = np.array([[[255, 0, 0, 157]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    before = source.copy()
    result = adjustments.hsl(source, 0, 0, saturation, lightness)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(result[0, 0, :3], expected, atol=1)
    assert result[0, 0, 3] == 157
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(source, before)


def test_hsl_edits_only_selected_hue_and_keeps_gray_neutral():
    """A red selection lightens red; green, blue and uncolored pixels stay unchanged."""
    source = np.array([[[255, 0, 0, 255], [0, 255, 0, 255],
                        [0, 0, 255, 255], [80, 80, 80, 255]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    result = adjustments.hsl(source, 0, 0, 0, 25)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(result[0, 0, :3], [255, 128, 128], atol=1)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[0, 1:], source[0, 1:])


@pytest.mark.parametrize('gamma', [.1, .5, 1., 2., 3.])
def test_main_gamma_matches_direct_power_transform(gamma):
    """Both gamma controls must use s=255(r/255)^gamma, with 100 meaning gamma=1."""
    values = np.arange(256, dtype=np.uint8).reshape(16, 16)
    source = np.dstack((values, values, values, 255 - values))
    result = adjustments.adjust(source, {'gamma': gamma * 100})
    expected = enhancement_point.gamma(source, gamma)
    np.testing.assert_allclose(result, expected, atol=1)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], source[..., 3])

"""Tích hợp bộ lọc, tham số, worker, lịch sử, tài liệu và dialog Qt."""
import time

import numpy as np
import pytest
from PySide6.QtCore import QThread
from PySide6.QtTest import QTest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QDialog

from app.core import enhancement
from app.ui import lesson_tools
from app.ui import window as window_module
from app.ui.lesson_tools import LessonDialog, AnalysisPreview
from app.ui.window import MainWindow


@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def qt_app():
    return QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])


def wait_done(app, window):
    deadline = time.monotonic() + 15
    while window.busy and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        app.processEvents()
        QTest.qWait(10)
    assert not window.busy


@pytest.fixture
def editor(qt_app, rgba):
    window = MainWindow()
    window.set_document(rgba, 'bai_hoc.png')
    window.errors = []
    window.alert = window.errors.append
    yield window
    wait_done(qt_app, window)
    for preview in window.findChildren(AnalysisPreview):
        preview.close()
    window.reset_sliders()
    window.doc.mark_saved()
    window.close()


def choice(algorithm, params=None, chapter=2, **kwargs):
    return {'chapter': chapter, 'algorithm': algorithm, 'params': params or {},
            'mode': 'rgb', 'reference': None, 'frames': [], **kwargs}


def select(dialog, chapter, key):
    dialog.chapter.setCurrentIndex(dialog.chapter.findData(chapter))
    dialog.group.setCurrentText(dialog.modules[chapter].ALGORITHMS[key].group)
    dialog.algorithm.setCurrentIndex(dialog.algorithm.findData(key))


def test_dynamic_dialog_precision_and_required_images(qt_app, monkeypatch):
    dialog = LessonDialog()
    try:
        assert dialog.chapter.count() == 3
        select(dialog, 2, 'inverse')
        epsilon = dialog.widgets['epsilon']
        assert epsilon.minimum() == .0001
        epsilon.setValue(.0001)
        assert dialog.selection()['params']['epsilon'] == .0001
        select(dialog, 2, 'frequency_low')
        dialog.widgets['cutoff'].setValue(.001)
        assert dialog.selection()['params']['cutoff'] == .001
        select(dialog, 2, 'negative')
        assert dialog.selection()['params'] == {}
        assert not dialog.mode_row.isHidden()
        select(dialog, 2, 'otsu')
        assert dialog.mode_row.isHidden()
        select(dialog, 2, 'average')
        dialog.accept()
        assert dialog.result() == QDialog.DialogCode.Rejected
        assert 'ít nhất một ảnh' in dialog.error.text()
        select(dialog, 2, 'match')
        dialog.accept()
        assert 'tham chiếu' in dialog.error.text()
        monkeypatch.setattr(lesson_tools.QFileDialog, 'getOpenFileName', lambda *a, **kw: ('/tmp/reference.png', ''))
        dialog.choose_reference()
        assert dialog.selection()['reference'] == '/tmp/reference.png'
        select(dialog, 2, 'negative')
        assert dialog.selection()['reference'] is None
        dialog.accept()
        assert dialog.result() == QDialog.DialogCode.Accepted
    finally:
        dialog.close()


def test_named_channels_and_translated_parameters_route_real_values(qt_app, rgba):
    """Tên kênh rõ ràng phải chọn đúng dữ liệu, không gửi nhãn dịch vào core."""
    dialog = LessonDialog()
    try:
        assert 'Bài học' not in dialog.windowTitle()
        assert all('Chương' not in dialog.chapter.itemText(i) for i in range(dialog.chapter.count()))
        select(dialog, 1, 'channel')
        channel = dialog.widgets['channel']
        assert channel.itemText(0) == 'Đỏ (R)'
        channel.setCurrentIndex(1)
        result, _ = lesson_tools.process_lesson(rgba, dialog.selection())
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 0], rgba[..., 1])
        dialog.widgets['space'].setCurrentIndex(dialog.widgets['space'].findData('HSV'))
        assert channel.currentText() == 'Độ bão hòa (S)'
        assert dialog.selection()['params'] == {'space': 'HSV', 'channel': 1}
        select(dialog, 1, 'arithmetic')
        operation = dialog.widgets['operation']
        operation.setCurrentIndex(operation.findData('difference'))
        assert operation.currentText() == 'Chênh lệch tuyệt đối'
        assert dialog.selection()['params']['operation'] == 'difference'
        select(dialog, 2, 'negative')
        assert dialog.selection()['mode'] == 'rgb'
        result, _ = lesson_tools.process_lesson(rgba, dialog.selection())
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., :3], 255 - rgba[..., :3])
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], rgba[..., 3])
        select(dialog, 2, 'gaussian')
        assert dialog.selection()['mode'] == 'luminance'
    finally:
        dialog.close()


def test_only_effective_filter_parameters_are_editable(qt_app):
    """Tham số không được thuật toán sử dụng phải rõ trạng thái trên form."""
    dialog = LessonDialog()
    try:
        select(dialog, 2, 'frequency_low')
        assert not dialog.widgets['order'].isEnabled()
        family = dialog.widgets['family']
        family.setCurrentIndex(family.findData('butterworth'))
        assert dialog.widgets['order'].isEnabled()
        assert dialog.selection()['params']['family'] == 'butterworth'
        family.setCurrentIndex(family.findData('ideal'))
        assert not dialog.widgets['order'].isEnabled()
        select(dialog, 2, 'inverse')
        assert dialog.widgets['sigma'].isEnabled()
        dialog.widgets['psf'].setCurrentIndex(dialog.widgets['psf'].findData('motion'))
        assert not dialog.widgets['sigma'].isEnabled()
        select(dialog, 2, 'nearest')
        dialog.widgets['size'].setValue(3)
        assert dialog.widgets['neighbors'].maximum() == 9
        dialog.widgets['size'].setValue(5)
        assert dialog.widgets['neighbors'].maximum() == 25
        dialog.widgets['size'].setValue(4)
        dialog.accept()
        assert dialog.result() == QDialog.DialogCode.Rejected
        assert 'phải là số lẻ' in dialog.error.text()
    finally:
        dialog.close()


def test_lesson_guards_and_cancel_preserve_document(editor, rgba, monkeypatch):
    calls = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'choose_lesson', lambda parent: calls.append(parent))
    editor.do_lesson()
    assert calls == [editor] and not editor.busy
    editor.sliders['brightness'].setValue(10)
    editor.do_lesson()
    assert calls == [editor] and editor.errors
    assert len(editor.doc.entries) == 1
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current, rgba)
    editor.reset_sliders()
    old_document = editor.doc
    editor.doc = None
    editor.do_lesson()
    assert len(calls) == 1 and 'mở ảnh' in editor.errors[-1]
    editor.doc = old_document


def test_no_parameter_lesson_commits_alpha_histogram_and_undo(editor, qt_app, rgba, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'choose_lesson', lambda parent: choice('negative'))
    original_histogram = editor.histogram.values.copy()
    editor.do_lesson()
    wait_done(qt_app, editor)
    expected = rgba.copy()
    expected[..., :3] = 255 - expected[..., :3]
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current, expected)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.histogram.reference_values, original_histogram)
    assert not editor.errors and len(editor.doc.entries) == 2
    assert 'Âm bản' in editor.histogram_context and 'Chương' not in editor.histogram_context
    editor.undo()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current, rgba)
    editor.redo()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current, expected)


def test_lesson_reference_and_frames_load_in_worker(editor, qt_app, rgba, monkeypatch):
    second = rgba.copy()
    second[..., :3] //= 2
    loads = []

    def load(path):
        loads.append(path)
        assert QThread.currentThread() != qt_app.thread()
        return second.copy()

    monkeypatch.setattr(lesson_tools, 'load_image', load)
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'choose_lesson', lambda parent: choice('match', reference='reference.png'))
    editor.do_lesson()
    wait_done(qt_app, editor)
    expected, _ = enhancement.process(rgba, 'match', mode='rgb', reference=second)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current, expected)
    assert loads == ['reference.png'] and not editor.errors
    editor.undo()
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'choose_lesson', lambda parent: choice('average', frames=['a.png', 'b.png']))
    editor.do_lesson()
    wait_done(qt_app, editor)
    expected, _ = enhancement.process(rgba, 'average', mode='rgb', frames=[second, second])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current, expected)
    assert loads == ['reference.png', 'a.png', 'b.png'] and not editor.errors


@pytest.mark.parametrize('chapter', [1, 3])
def test_other_chapters_route_without_color_mode(editor, qt_app, rgba, monkeypatch, chapter):
    module = lesson_tools.lesson_modules()[chapter]
    key = next(iter(module.ALGORITHMS))
    received = []

    def process(image, algorithm, params=None):
        assert QThread.currentThread() != qt_app.thread()
        received.append((algorithm, params))
        result = image.copy()
        result[..., :3] = 77
        return result, 'Thông tin kết quả'

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'process', process)
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'choose_lesson', lambda parent: choice(key, {'value': 7}, chapter=chapter))
    editor.do_lesson()
    wait_done(qt_app, editor)
    assert received == [(key, {'value': 7})]
    assert not editor.errors and len(editor.doc.entries) == 2
    assert 'Thông tin kết quả' in editor.doc.entries[-1][1]
    assert 'Thông tin kết quả' in editor.hint.text()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current[..., 3], rgba[..., 3])


def test_chapter_one_receives_second_image_in_worker(editor, qt_app, rgba, monkeypatch):
    module = lesson_tools.lesson_modules()[1]
    key = next(key for key, spec in module.ALGORITHMS.items() if spec.reference)
    second = rgba[:, ::-1].copy()
    received = []

    def process(image, algorithm, params=None, reference=None):
        assert QThread.currentThread() != qt_app.thread()
        received.append((algorithm, reference))
        return image.copy(), ''

    monkeypatch.setattr(module, 'process', process)
    monkeypatch.setattr(lesson_tools, 'load_image', lambda path: second.copy())
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'choose_lesson',
                        lambda parent: choice(key, chapter=1, reference='second.png'))
    editor.do_lesson()
    wait_done(qt_app, editor)
    assert not editor.errors and received[0][0] == key
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(received[0][1], second)


def test_diagnostic_and_worker_error_leave_history_untouched(editor, qt_app, rgba, monkeypatch):
    counts = editor.histogram.values.copy()
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'choose_lesson', lambda parent: choice('spectrum'))
    editor.do_lesson()
    wait_done(qt_app, editor)
    previews = editor.findChildren(AnalysisPreview)
    assert len(previews) == 1 and previews[0].isVisible()
    assert len(editor.doc.entries) == 1 and not editor.doc.dirty
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.histogram.values, counts)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current, rgba)
    previews[0].close()
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'choose_lesson', lambda parent: choice('gamma', {'gamma': -1.}))
    editor.do_lesson()
    wait_done(qt_app, editor)
    assert len(editor.errors) == 1 and editor.centralWidget().isEnabled()
    assert len(editor.doc.entries) == 1
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(editor.doc.current, rgba)


def test_quality_metrics_run_in_worker_without_commit(editor, qt_app, rgba, monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module.QFileDialog, 'getOpenFileName', lambda *a, **kw: ('reference.png', ''))

    def load(path):
        assert QThread.currentThread() != qt_app.thread()
        return rgba.copy()

    results = []
    monkeypatch.setattr(window_module, 'load_image', load)
    monkeypatch.setattr(editor, 'show_quality', lambda result, name: results.append((result, name)))
    editor.do_quality()
    wait_done(qt_app, editor)
    assert results[0][1] == 'reference.png'
    assert results[0][0]['mse'] == 0 and np.isinf(results[0][0]['psnr'])
    assert results[0][0]['ssim'] == pytest.approx(1.)
    assert len(editor.doc.entries) == 1 and not editor.doc.dirty and not editor.errors


=== ./tests/test_analysis_dispatcher.py ===

"""Registry parameters must reach the existing analysis algorithms unchanged."""
import numpy as np
import pytest

from app.core import analysis


def gray_image(values):
    gray = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.uint8)
    return np.dstack((gray, gray, gray, np.full(gray.shape, 255, dtype=np.uint8)))


@pytest.mark.parametrize('algorithm', list(analysis.ALGORITHMS))
def test_every_registered_algorithm_runs_with_its_dialog_defaults(algorithm):
    """The picker must import and run every entry without unsupported keyword errors."""
    image = gray_image(np.tile([20, 20, 90, 90, 160, 160, 220, 220], (8, 1)))
    before = image.copy()
    fields = analysis.fields_for_image(algorithm, image.shape)
    params = {key: value[0] if isinstance(value, list) else value
              for key, _, value, _, _ in fields}
    result, info = analysis.process(image, algorithm, params)
    assert result.dtype == np.uint8 and result.shape == image.shape
    assert isinstance(info, str)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], image[..., 3])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(image, before)


def test_region_coordinates_use_width_for_x_and_height_for_y():
    """A non-square image exposes swapped axes and rejects out-of-bounds seeds."""
    original = analysis.ALGORITHMS['region_grow'].fields
    fields = {key: (value, low, high) for key, _, value, low, high
              in analysis.fields_for_image('region_grow', (3, 7, 4))}
    assert fields['seed_x'] == (3, 0, 6)
    assert fields['seed_y'] == (1, 0, 2)
    assert analysis.ALGORITHMS['region_grow'].fields == original
    image = gray_image([[20] * 7] * 3)
    image[2, 6, :3] = 100
    result, info = analysis.process(image, 'region_grow', {'seed_x': 6, 'seed_y': 2, 'tolerance': 0})
    assert np.count_nonzero(result[..., 0]) == 1 and result[2, 6, 0] == 255
    assert '(6, 2)' in info
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        analysis.process(image, 'region_grow', {'seed_x': 7})
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        analysis.process(image, 'region_grow', {'seed_y': 3})


def test_morphology_operation_and_boolean_parameters_reach_core():
    image = gray_image([[0, 0, 0], [0, 255, 0], [0, 0, 0]])
    dilated, _ = analysis.process(image, 'dilate', {'shape': 'cross', 'grayscale': False})
    expected = np.array([[0, 255, 0], [255, 255, 255], [0, 255, 0]], dtype=np.uint8)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(dilated[..., 0], expected)
    eroded, _ = analysis.process(image, 'erode')
    assert not eroded[..., :3].any()


def test_region_information_is_not_discarded():
    image = gray_image([[0, 0, 255], [0, 0, 0], [255, 0, 0]])
    result, info = analysis.process(image, 'components', {'connectivity': 4, 'output': 'binary'})
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, image)
    assert '2 vùng' in info


def test_invalid_algorithm_and_parameters_raise_clear_errors():
    image = gray_image([[10]])
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='không tồn tại'):
        analysis.process(image, 'missing')
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match='Tham số'):
        analysis.process(image, 'prewitt', {'operation': 'wrong'})


=== ./tests/test_color_range.py ===

"""Kiểm thử lọc gam màu qua hue tuần hoàn và bảo toàn ảnh RGBA đầu vào."""
import numpy as np
import pytest

from app.core.filters import channel_view, color_range


@pytest.mark.parametrize('channel', [0, 1, 2])
def test_channel_view_preserves_component_not_only_hue(channel):
    """Màu hỗn hợp vẫn hiện cường độ kênh, hai kênh còn lại bằng 0."""
    image = np.array([[[210, 90, 65, 255], [170, 70, 50, 128],
                       [240, 190, 165, 0], [127, 127, 127, 255]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    original = image.copy()
    expected = np.zeros_like(image)
    expected[..., channel] = image[..., channel]
    expected[..., 3] = image[..., 3]
    result = channel_view(image, channel)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, expected)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(image, original)
    assert not np.shares_memory(result, image)


def test_channel_view_brightness_uses_histogram_weights():
    """Chỉ chế độ độ sáng tạo ảnh xám; RGB giữ nguyên alpha."""
    image = np.array([[[255, 0, 0, 3], [0, 255, 0, 128], [0, 0, 255, 255]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(channel_view(image, 3),
                                  [[[54, 54, 54, 3], [182, 182, 182, 128], [18, 18, 18, 255]]])


@pytest.mark.parametrize('channel', [-1, 4, None, 'red', 1.5])
def test_invalid_view_channel_raises(channel):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        channel_view(np.full((1, 1, 4), 255, dtype=np.uint8), channel)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('channel', [0, 1, 2])
def test_only_chosen_primary_keeps_its_color(channel):
    """Gam chính được giữ nguyên, hai gam khác thành xám có độ sáng biết trước."""
    image = np.array([[[255, 0, 0, 255], [0, 255, 0, 128], [0, 0, 255, 0]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    original = image.copy()
    expected = np.array([[[54, 54, 54, 255], [182, 182, 182, 128], [18, 18, 18, 0]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    expected[0, channel] = image[0, channel]
    result = color_range(image, channel)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, expected)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(image, original)
    assert result.dtype == np.uint8 and result.shape == image.shape
    assert not np.shares_memory(result, image)


def test_red_range_wraps_across_hue_zero():
    """Hai màu đỏ ở hai phía 0/360 độ vẫn có sắc đỏ rõ, xanh bị chuyển xám."""
    image = np.array([[[255, 8, 0, 255], [255, 0, 8, 255], [0, 255, 0, 255]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    result = color_range(image, 0)
    assert np.all(result[0, :2, 0] >= 250)
    assert np.all(result[0, :2, 1:3] <= 10)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[0, 2], [182, 182, 182, 255])


@pytest.mark.parametrize('channel', [0, 1, 2, 3])
def test_neutral_levels_and_alpha_remain_unchanged(channel):
    """Ảnh xám và các giá trị alpha không đổi khi bật bất kỳ gam màu nào."""
    image = np.array([[[0, 0, 0, 0], [27, 27, 27, 1], [128, 128, 128, 127],
                       [255, 255, 255, 255]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(color_range(image, channel), image)


def test_brightness_produces_expected_gray_levels():
    """Kênh độ sáng dùng trọng số Y' thống nhất với histogram và giữ alpha."""
    image = np.array([[[255, 0, 0, 3], [0, 255, 0, 128], [0, 0, 255, 255],
                       [255, 255, 255, 0]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    result = color_range(image, 3)
    expected = np.array([[[54, 54, 54, 3], [182, 182, 182, 128], [18, 18, 18, 255],
                          [255, 255, 255, 0]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result, expected)


def test_neighboring_hues_fade_gradually_to_gray():
    """Sắc cam trong gam đỏ được pha mềm, sắc vàng ở biên thành xám."""
    image = np.array([[[255, 0, 0, 255], [255, 128, 0, 255], [255, 255, 0, 255]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    result = color_range(image, 0)
    spread = np.ptp(result[0, :, :3].astype(int), axis=1)
    assert spread[0] == 255 and 0 < spread[1] < 255 and spread[2] == 0


@pytest.mark.parametrize('channel', [-1, 4, None, 'red', 1.5])
def test_invalid_color_channel_raises(channel):
    """Kênh không hợp lệ báo lỗi thay vì chọn nhầm gam màu."""
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        color_range(np.full((1, 1, 4), 255, dtype=np.uint8), channel)


=== ./tests/test_histogram.py ===

"""Kiểm tra số đếm histogram và đồng bộ với các thao tác chỉnh ảnh."""
import numpy as np
import pytest
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication

from app.core import adjustments, geometry
from app.ui.histogram import Histogram
from app.ui.window import MainWindow


@pytest.fixture(scope='module')
def qt_app():
    """Giữ QApplication trong suốt các kiểm thử widget của mô-đun."""
    return QApplication.instance() or QApplication([])


def test_rgb_and_brightness_counts_ignore_hidden_pixels(qt_app):
    """Màu cơ bản có đúng 256 bin; alpha nhỏ vẫn được đếm, alpha=0 bị bỏ."""
    image = np.array([[[0, 0, 0, 255], [255, 255, 255, 255],
                       [255, 0, 0, 255], [0, 255, 0, 255],
                       [0, 0, 255, 1], [17, 33, 49, 0]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    chart = Histogram()
    chart.set_image(image)
    expected = np.zeros((4, 256), dtype=np.int64)
    expected[:3, 0] = 3
    expected[:3, 255] = 2
    expected[3, [0, 255, 54, 182, 18]] = 1
    assert chart.pixel_count == 5
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(chart.values, expected)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(chart.values.sum(axis=1), [5] * 4)
    chart.close()


def test_reference_keeps_its_own_counts_and_can_be_cleared(qt_app):
    """So sánh ảnh khác kích thước giữ số đếm gốc và không sửa dữ liệu nguồn."""
    before = np.full((2, 3, 4), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    after = np.zeros((1, 2, 4), dtype=np.uint8)
    after[..., 3] = 255
    chart = Histogram()
    chart.set_reference(before)
    chart.set_context('Cắt ảnh')
    chart.set_image(after)
    assert chart.reference_pixel_count == 6
    assert chart.pixel_count == 2
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(chart.reference_values[:, 255], [6] * 4)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(chart.values[:, 0], [2] * 4)
    assert chart.context.text() == 'Cắt ảnh'
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(before, np.full((2, 3, 4), 255, dtype=np.uint8))
    counts = chart.values.copy()
    chart.set_reference(None)
    assert chart.reference_values is None and chart.reference_pixel_count == 0
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(chart.values, counts)
    chart.close()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('logarithmic', [False, True])
def test_enlarged_histogram_preserves_an_independent_snapshot(qt_app, logarithmic):
    """Biểu đồ phóng to giữ dữ liệu, thang và tên khi panel nguồn đổi ảnh."""
    source = Histogram()
    enlarged = Histogram()
    before = np.full((2, 3, 4), 255, dtype=np.uint8)
    after = np.array([[[30, 70, 120, 255], [90, 10, 25, 255]]], dtype=np.uint8)
    source.set_reference(before)
    source.set_image(after)
    source.set_context('Tăng tương phản')
    source.log_scale.setChecked(logarithmic)
    source.set_filter_mode('gamut')
    enlarged.copy_from(source)
    counts, reference = source.values.copy(), source.reference_values.copy()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(enlarged.values, counts)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(enlarged.reference_values, reference)
    assert not np.shares_memory(enlarged.values, source.values)
    assert not np.shares_memory(enlarged.reference_values, source.reference_values)
    assert enlarged.pixel_count == 2 and enlarged.reference_pixel_count == 6
    assert enlarged.context.text() == 'Tăng tương phản'
    assert enlarged.sample_label.text() == source.sample_label.text()
    assert enlarged.log_scale.isChecked() == logarithmic
    assert enlarged.filter_mode == 'gamut'
    assert enlarged.filter_mode_selector.currentData() == 'gamut'

    source.set_image(before)
    source.set_reference(None)
    source.set_context('Ảnh khác')
    source.log_scale.setChecked(not logarithmic)
    source.set_filter_mode('channel')
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(enlarged.values, counts)
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(enlarged.reference_values, reference)
    assert enlarged.context.text() == 'Tăng tương phản'
    assert enlarged.log_scale.isChecked() == logarithmic
    assert enlarged.filter_mode == 'gamut'
    empty = Histogram()
    enlarged.copy_from(empty)
    assert enlarged.values is None and enlarged.reference_values is None
    assert enlarged.pixel_count == 0 and enlarged.reference_pixel_count == 0
    source.close()
    enlarged.close()
    empty.close()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('logarithmic', [False, True])
def test_bar_charts_render_empty_and_transparent_images(qt_app, logarithmic):
    """Cả hai thang vẽ được khi chưa mở ảnh và không có pixel nhìn thấy."""
    chart = Histogram()
    chart.resize(340, chart.height())
    chart.log_scale.setChecked(logarithmic)
    chart.show()
    qt_app.processEvents()
    assert not chart.grab().isNull()
    transparent = np.full((2, 3, 4), 127, dtype=np.uint8)
    transparent[..., 3] = 0
    chart.set_reference(transparent)
    chart.set_image(transparent)
    qt_app.processEvents()
    assert chart.pixel_count == 0 and chart.reference_pixel_count == 0
    assert not np.any(chart.values) and not np.any(chart.reference_values)
    assert not chart.grab().isNull()
    chart.close()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('logarithmic', [False, True])
def test_rendered_histogram_contains_colored_bars(qt_app, logarithmic):
    """Các cột RGB thật sự được vẽ, đổi thang không làm thay đổi số đếm."""
    image = np.zeros((1, 256, 4), dtype=np.uint8)
    image[..., :3] = np.arange(256, dtype=np.uint8)[None, :, None]
    image[..., 3] = 255
    chart = Histogram()
    chart.resize(1400, chart.height())
    chart.set_image(image)
    counts = chart.values.copy()
    chart.set_reference(image[:, :128])
    chart.log_scale.setChecked(logarithmic)
    chart.show()
    qt_app.processEvents()
    for channel in chart.charts[:3]:
        rendered = channel.grab().toImage()
        color = channel.color
        assert any(rendered.pixelColor(x, y) == color
                   for y in range(rendered.height())
                   for x in range(rendered.width()))
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(chart.values, counts)
    chart.close()


def test_preview_compare_reset_and_history_histograms(qt_app):
    """Biểu đồ theo đúng preview, ảnh gốc, hoàn tác và từng chức năng trong lịch sử."""
    image = np.empty((4, 6, 4), dtype=np.uint8)
    image[:] = [50, 70, 90, 255]
    window = MainWindow()
    window.set_document(image, 'histogram.png')
    try:
        original_counts = window.histogram.values.copy()
        window.sliders['brightness'].setValue(20)
        window.preview()
        window.timer.stop()
        preview_counts = window.histogram.values.copy()
        assert not np.array_equal(preview_counts, original_counts)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.reference_values, original_counts)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.doc.current, image)
        window.show_original(True)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, original_counts)
        window.show_original(False)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, preview_counts)
        window.reset_adjustments()
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, original_counts)

        brighter = adjustments.adjust(image, {'brightness': 20})
        window.commit(brighter, 'Tăng sáng')
        brighter_counts = window.histogram.values.copy()
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.reference_values, original_counts)
        cropped = geometry.crop(brighter, (0, 0, 3, 2))
        window.commit(cropped, 'Cắt ảnh')
        assert window.histogram.pixel_count == 6
        assert window.histogram.reference_pixel_count == 24
        cropped_counts = window.histogram.values.copy()

        window.history.setCurrentRow(1)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, brighter_counts)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.reference_values, original_counts)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.doc.current, cropped)
        assert window.canvas.photo.pixmap().size().width() == 3
        assert window.doc.index == 2
        window.show_original(True)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, original_counts)
        window.show_original(False)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, brighter_counts)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.reference_values, original_counts)
        assert window.history.currentRow() == 1
        assert window.canvas.photo.pixmap().size().width() == 3
        window.history.setCurrentRow(0)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, original_counts)
        assert window.histogram.reference_values is None
        window.undo()
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, brighter_counts)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.reference_values, original_counts)
        window.redo()
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.values, cropped_counts)
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(window.histogram.reference_values, brighter_counts)
    finally:
        window.reset_sliders()
        window.doc.mark_saved()
        window.close()


=== ./tests/test_analysis_edges.py ===

"""Mặt nạ Chương 3, đổi dấu LoG và đường thẳng tổng hợp để kiểm thử Hough."""
import numpy as np
import pytest

from app.core import analysis_edges as edges


def rgba_gray(values):
    gray = np.asarray(values, dtype=np.uint8)
    return np.dstack((gray, gray, gray, np.full(gray.shape, 255, dtype=np.uint8)))


def test_roberts_textbook_diagonal_masks_and_magnitude():
    """Biên 20|200 ở trang 10 có Gx=-180, Gy=180, độ lớn 254.56."""
    image = rgba_gray([[20, 20, 200, 200]] * 4)
    assert edges.gradient(image, 'roberts', 'x')[1, 1, 0] == 180
    assert edges.gradient(image, 'roberts', 'y')[1, 1, 0] == 180
    assert edges.gradient(image, 'roberts')[1, 1, 0] == 255
    assert edges.gradient(image, 'roberts')[1, 0, 0] == 0


def test_prewitt_equal_weights_and_signed_response_before_absolute():
    """Ba hàng cùng trọng số: biên 70|20 có Gx=-150, Gy=0."""
    image = rgba_gray([[70, 70, 20, 20, 20]] * 5)
    assert edges.gradient(image, 'prewitt', 'x')[2, 1, 0] == 150
    assert edges.gradient(image, 'prewitt', 'y')[2, 1, 0] == 0
    assert edges.gradient(image, 'prewitt')[2, 1, 0] == 150


def test_zero_crossing_selects_single_nearest_endpoint_and_exact_zero():
    response = np.array([[5., 3., -1., -5., -5.]])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(edges._zero_crossings(response, 2), [[False, False, True, False, False]])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(edges._zero_crossings(response, 5), np.zeros((1, 5), dtype=bool))
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(edges._zero_crossings(np.array([[2., 0., -2.]]), 1), [[False, True, False]])
    assert not edges._zero_crossings(np.array([[0., 0., 2., 2.]]), 0).any()


def test_log_step_edges_are_thin_and_constant_region_has_no_edges():
    image = rgba_gray(np.pad(np.full((16, 16), 255, dtype=np.uint8), ((0, 0), (16, 0))))
    result = edges.log_zero_crossing(image, sigma=1, threshold=5)
    columns = np.where(np.any(result[..., 0] > 0, axis=0))[0]
    assert len(columns) == 1
    assert columns[0] in (15, 16)
    assert not edges.log_zero_crossing(image, threshold=2040)[..., :3].any()


@pytest.mark.parametrize('mode', ['standard', 'probabilistic'])
def test_hough_marks_known_vertical_line_and_keeps_other_colors(mode):
    gray = np.zeros((96, 96), dtype=np.uint8)
    gray[8:88, 45] = 255
    image = rgba_gray(gray)
    result = edges.hough_lines(image, mode=mode, threshold=35, min_length=50, max_gap=2, line_width=1)
    red = np.all(result[..., :3] == (255, 64, 64), axis=2)
    assert red[:, 43:48].sum() >= 50
    assert not red[:, :20].any()
    if mode == 'probabilistic':
        np.testing.assert_array_equal(edges.hough_lines(image, threshold=35, min_length=200), image)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('shape', [(1, 1), (1, 7), (7, 1), (7, 8)])
def test_constant_and_tiny_images(shape):
    image = rgba_gray(np.full(shape, 80, dtype=np.uint8))
    assert not edges.gradient(image)[..., :3].any()
    assert not edges.gradient(image, 'prewitt')[..., :3].any()
    assert not edges.log_zero_crossing(image)[..., :3].any()
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(edges.hough_lines(image), image)


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function', [edges.gradient, edges.log_zero_crossing, edges.hough_lines])
def test_rgba_immutable_and_hidden_colors_do_not_affect_visible_output(rgba, function):
    original = rgba.copy()
    result = function(rgba)
    assert result.shape == rgba.shape and result.dtype == np.uint8
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(result[..., 3], rgba[..., 3])
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(rgba, original)
    changed = rgba.copy()
    changed[changed[..., 3] == 0, :3] = 0
    visible = rgba[..., 3] > 0
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(function(changed)[visible], result[visible])
    hidden = rgba.copy()
    hidden[..., 3] = 0
    np.testing.assert_array_equal(function(hidden), hidden)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        function(rgba[..., :3])


@pytest.mark.parametrize('function,kwargs', [
    (edges.gradient, {'kind': 'sobel'}), (edges.gradient, {'direction': 'z'}),
    (edges.log_zero_crossing, {'sigma': 0}), (edges.log_zero_crossing, {'sigma': np.nan}),
    (edges.log_zero_crossing, {'threshold': -1}),
    (edges.hough_lines, {'mode': 'other'}), (edges.hough_lines, {'threshold': 0}),
    (edges.hough_lines, {'threshold': 2.3}), (edges.hough_lines, {'min_length': -1}),
    (edges.hough_lines, {'max_gap': np.inf}), (edges.hough_lines, {'line_width': 0}),
    (edges.hough_lines, {'max_lines': 0}), (edges.hough_lines, {'canny_low': 150}),
])
def test_invalid_parameters_are_rejected(function, kwargs):
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        function(rgba_gray([[40]]), **kwargs)
